- **visual-iteration**: Let an AI SEE what it builds and fix it. Render -&gt; look -&gt; compare-to-reference -&gt; edit -&gt; re-render loop for getting web design (layout, CSS, spacing, typography, color) or 3D/game scenes (character look, mesh, texture, placement, lighting, camera framing) visually correct.
